    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(

                )
                connect_request = Request(

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(

                )
                connect_request = Request(
                    method=b"CONNECT",
                    url=connect_url,
                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )
                connect_response = self._connection.handle_request(
                    connect_request
                )
    
                if connect_response.status < 200 or connect_response.status > 299:
                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

request = <Request [b'HEAD']>

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(
                    [(b"Host", target), (b"Accept", b"*/*")], self._proxy_headers
                )
                connect_request = Request(
                    method=b"CONNECT",
                    url=connect_url,
                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )
                connect_response = self._connection.handle_request(
                    connect_request
                )
    
                if connect_response.status < 200 or connect_response.status > 299:
                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")
                    reason_str = reason_bytes.decode("ascii", errors="ignore")
                    msg = "%d %s" % (connect_response.status, reason_str)
                    self._connection.close()
>                   raise ProxyError(msg)

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

request = <Request [b'HEAD']>

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(
                    [(b"Host", target), (b"Accept", b"*/*")], self._proxy_headers
                )
                connect_request = Request(
                    method=b"CONNECT",
                    url=connect_url,
                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )
                connect_response = self._connection.handle_request(
                    connect_request
                )
    
                if connect_response.status < 200 or connect_response.status > 299:
                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")
                    reason_str = reason_bytes.decode("ascii", errors="ignore")
                    msg = "%d %s" % (connect_response.status, reason_str)
                    self._connection.close()
>                   raise ProxyError(msg)

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

request = <Request [b'HEAD']>

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(
                    [(b"Host", target), (b"Accept", b"*/*")], self._proxy_headers
                )
                connect_request = Request(
                    method=b"CONNECT",
                    url=connect_url,
                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )
                connect_response = self._connection.handle_request(
                    connect_request
                )
    
                if connect_response.status < 200 or connect_response.status > 299:
                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")
                    reason_str = reason_bytes.decode("ascii", errors="ignore")
                    msg = "%d %s" % (connect_response.status, reason_str)
                    self._connection.close()
>                   raise ProxyError(msg)

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(
                    [(b"Host", target), (b"Accept", b"*/*")], self._proxy_headers
                )
                connect_request = Request(
                    method=b"CONNECT",
                    url=connect_url,
                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )
                connect_response = self._connection.handle_request(
                    connect_request
                )
    
                if connect_response.status < 200 or connect_response.status > 299:
                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")
                    reason_str = reason_bytes.decode("ascii", errors="ignore")
                    msg = "%d %s" % (connect_response.status, reason_str)
                    self._connection.close()
>                   raise ProxyError(msg)

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(
                    [(b"Host", target), (b"Accept", b"*/*")], self._proxy_headers
                )
                connect_request = Request(
                    method=b"CONNECT",
                    url=connect_url,
                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )
                connect_response = self._connection.handle_request(
                    connect_request
                )
    
                if connect_response.status < 200 or connect_response.status > 299:
                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")
                    reason_str = reason_bytes.decode("ascii", errors="ignore")
                    msg = "%d %s" % (connect_response.status, reason_str)
                    self._connection.close()
>                   raise ProxyError(msg)

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(
                    [(b"Host", target), (b"Accept", b"*/*")], self._proxy_headers
                )
                connect_request = Request(
                    method=b"CONNECT",
                    url=connect_url,
                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )
                connect_response = self._connection.handle_request(
                    connect_request
                )
    
                if connect_response.status < 200 or connect_response.status > 299:
                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")
                    reason_str = reason_bytes.decode("ascii", errors="ignore")
                    msg = "%d %s" % (connect_response.status, reason_str)
                    self._connection.close()
>                   raise ProxyError(msg)

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(
                    [(b"Host", target), (b"Accept", b"*/*")], self._proxy_headers
                )
                connect_request = Request(
                    method=b"CONNECT",
                    url=connect_url,
                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )
                connect_response = self._connection.handle_request(
                    connect_request
                )
    
                if connect_response.status < 200 or connect_response.status > 299:
                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")
                    reason_str = reason_bytes.decode("ascii", errors="ignore")
                    msg = "%d %s" % (connect_response.status, reason_str)
                    self._connection.close()

    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP

            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()

                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,

                )

                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )

                    connect_request
                )

                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )
                connect_headers = merge_headers(
                    [(b"Host", target), (b"Accept", b"*/*")], self._proxy_headers
                )
                connect_request = Request(
                    method=b"CONNECT",
                    url=connect_url,
                    headers=connect_headers,
                    extensions=request.extensions,
                )
                connect_response = self._connection.handle_request(
                    connect_request
                )
    
                if connect_response.status < 200 or connect_response.status > 299:
                    reason_bytes = connect_response.extensions.get("reason_phrase", b"")
                    reason_str = reason_bytes.decode("ascii", errors="ignore")
                    msg = "%d %s" % (connect_response.status, reason_str)
                    self._connection.close()

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc
E           httpx.ProxyError: 403 Forbidden

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc

    def handle_request(self, request: Request) -> Response:
        timeouts = request.extensions.get("timeout", {})
        timeout = timeouts.get("connect", None)
    
        with self._connect_lock:
            if not self._connected:
                target = b"%b:%d" % (self._remote_origin.host, self._remote_origin.port)
    
                connect_url = URL(
                    scheme=self._proxy_origin.scheme,
                    host=self._proxy_origin.host,
                    port=self._proxy_origin.port,
                    target=target,
                )

    def test_session_resumes_after_simulated_backend_restart_via_live_websocket(client):
        """End-to-end: create a session, take one real turn over a live WebSocket
        connection, disconnect, simulate a backend process restart (clear the
        in-memory SESSIONS registry -- the actual mechanism a real restart would
        wipe), reconnect on a NEW WebSocket connection with the SAME session_id,
        and confirm the session is transparently restored and can continue,
        rather than returning "Session not found"."""
        create_resp = client.post(
            "/api/sessions",
            json={
                "c_topics": ["Arrays"],
                "dsa_topics": [],
                "duration_minutes": 30,
                "num_questions": 5,
                "interview_mode": "standard",
            },
        )
        assert create_resp.status_code == 200
        session_id = create_resp.json()["id"]
        assert session_id in SESSIONS
    
        # --- First connection: start the session and answer one question ---
        with client.websocket_connect(f"/ws/interview/{session_id}") as ws:
            ws.send_json({"type": "start"})
            question_msg = ws.receive_json()
            assert question_msg["type"] == "question"
            question_id = question_msg["payload"]["id"]
    
            ws.send_json({
                "type": "voice_answer",
                "payload": {
                    "transcript": "I would use a hash map to store elements and their indices as I iterate.",
                    "question_id": question_id,
                    "attempts": 1,
                },
            })
            feedback_msg = ws.receive_json()
            assert feedback_msg["type"] == "feedback"
        # WebSocket context manager exit closes the connection (simulates a client disconnect).
    
        scores_after_first_turn = list(SESSIONS[session_id]._state["scores"])
